At first iteration prologue committed 1; then prefetch commits next (2 total) -> wait_prior(1). At last iteration no prefetch, outstanding 1 -> wait_prior(0) should wait for current. For multi-GPU 4 devices: for N=512 with 32x32 tile, blocks=256 total; splitting rows into 4 -> 64 blocks each. Each GPU: 64 blocks / (3 blocks per SM) ≈ 22 SMs used. Total 88 SMs across 4 GPUs vs single 82 SMs (256 blocks / 3 per SM ~ 85 SMs, all used). So multi-GPU yields no speedup for N=512: each GPU does 1/4 work but uses ~1/4 SMs. Time same. For N=1024: blocks=1024; single uses all 82 SMs with ~12.5 blocks/SM (multiple waves); multi each 256 blocks uses 82 SMs => ~3.1 blocks/SM; time ~1/3.4. Speedup near 4x. So multi-GPU helps for N≥1024. For N=512 neutral. Good: we can always use multi-GPU; for N=512 no loss except copies (but we generate data on device in prepare, so only C copy back ~2MB total split across GPUs; small). Actually with device-side init in prepare, no A/B copies timed; only C copy. Multi-GPU C copy in parallel-ish. So no downside.\n\nWait, for multi-GPU with row split and 32x32 tiles, each GPU gets N/4 rows. Grid = ceil(rows/32)*ceil(N/32). For N=512, rows=128 -> 4*16=64 blocks. With 16 accumulators and full unroll, should schedule well.\n\nTry a 4x4 variant where we use 128 threads per block by halving tile? no. Try 32x32 tile with 8x8 micro (16 threads) - bad. Try t8x4 with 32 threads (one warp): worse. Try t4x8 32 threads: worse.\n\nWhat about 32x32 tile with 64 threads but 2x2 warps arranged differently? Already.\n\nMaybe try 32x32 with BK=64 and 128 threads by having each thread compute 4x4 over only half the k? No, that's split-K within block requiring reduction. Could have 2 warps per output microtile each doing half k, then combine via shared: increases parallelism within block but same SM work. Not needed.\n\nTry 4x8 microtile (TM=8, TN=4 => acc 32, thread grid 4x8=32 threads) was bad due to one warp/block? Actually t8x4 with 32 threads gave worse. With t4x4 64 threads best. Try 4x4 but 32x64 tile (128 threads) gave 560 at 1024 (worse). So smaller tile 32x32 with 64 threads is good because more blocks and 2 warps each.\n\nTry 16x16 tile t4x4 (16 threads = half warp) - bad likely, but maybe many blocks. Skip.\n\nCould use 48x48 tile with 144 threads (4.5 warps) maybe worse.\n\nLet's test cp.async double buffering for 32x32 bk32 (shared 2*16KB=32KB, 3 blocks/SM) or bk64 (2*32=64KB, 1 block). BK32 double buffer could improve by overlapping loads with compute, but BK32 already comparable to bk64 (630 vs 629) at large N; bk64 better at small. Double buffering BK32 might beat both. Let's implement.\n\nAlso, maybe use `cp.async` single buffer BK64 then compute? No overlap.\n\nDouble-buffer kernel with cp.async, transposed A layout. cp.async copies 8 bytes to shared. Writing transposed means each element's destination is As[c*BM+r]; cp.async src global A[(row0+r)*K+k0+c] contiguous in c. Destination As[c*BM+r] for consecutive c is strided. cp.async works per 4/8/16 bytes with arbitrary shared address (must be aligned to size). 8-byte copies with shared address aligned 8. Fine.\n\nBut using cp.async for transposed writes means one instruction per element (8 bytes), same count as scalar loads; benefit is no register staging and async overlap. Could also use 16-byte cp.async loading two consecutive c into two shared locations? Destination not contiguous. No.\n\nAlternatively change shared layout for A to non-transposed (A[BM][BK]) and use cp.async 16-byte copies along c, but then compute loads a[m] strided (scalar). Tradeoff. Non-transposed with BK64: As[r*(BK+1)+c], can cp.async 16 bytes (double2) along c, coalesced global and shared (if padding stride multiple of 2; use BK+2? alignment). Compute needs 4 scalar a loads + vector b. Extra shared load instructions. Previous non-transposed bench2 32x32 t4x4 bk64 with 64 threads gave 495 at 1024, 549 at 2048, vs transposed 605/629. So transposed much better. Keep transposed.\n\nLet's test double-buffer with cp.async transposed and BK32/64. Implementation details: use `__pipeline_memcpy_async` from <cuda_pipeline.h> maybe easiest. It has signature `void __pipeline_memcpy_async(void* dst_shared, const void* src_global, size_t size_and_align)` where size 4,8,16. It requires dst/src alignment. For invalid bounds, use zero fill not supported; we can branch and write 0.0 directly (st.shared) for invalid. Since N default multiple of 32/64, avoid branch cost for interior? We can handle with condition. Mixing direct stores and cp.async to the same shared buffer then syncthreads after wait should be fine as long as order: direct stores happen before __syncthreads, and cp.async completion awaited. But there is potential race: cp.async writes could complete after direct stores; they target different addresses (invalid vs valid), so fine.\n\nDouble buffer prologue: load tile 0 for both A and B using cp.async, commit. Loop:\n```\nfor(int k0=0; k0<K; k0+=BK, buf^=1){\n  if (k0+BK < K) { loadTile(buf^1, k0+BK); }\n  __pipeline_commit();  // commit prefetch group (or commit empty? commits break group)\n  __pipeline_wait_prior(1); // ensure current (group before prefetch) done\n  __syncthreads();\n  compute(buf,k0);\n  __syncthreads();\n}\n```\nCareful: At prologue, load tile0, commit (group0). In iteration 0, prefetch tile1 and commit (group1); wait_prior(1) waits until only 1 group outstanding -> group0 done. Good. But if we call __pipeline_commit unconditionally even when no prefetch, then group count increments with empty group; wait_prior(1) would leave the empty latest group outstanding and current done—fine. At last iteration, after prefetch none, commit empty group2, wait_prior(1) => group1 done (current). Fine. So commit unconditionally works. But note __pipeline_commit even with no memcpy creates empty group. Good.\n\nAt end, no need to wait.\n\nBut __pipeline_wait_prior is per-thread; all threads call. Then syncthreads.\n\nLet's implement with __pipeline_memcpy_async. Need <cuda_pipeline.h>. Check availability in CUDA 12. nvcc supports. Alternatively inline asm `cp.async.ca.shared.global`. A[(size_t)gr*K+gc] : 0.0;\n    }\n    for (int e=threadIdx.x; e<K_TILE*TILE; e+=THREADS) {\n      int r=e/TILE,c=e%TILE;\n      int br=k0+r, bc=col0+c;\n      Bs[r*TILE+c] = (br<K && bc<N) ? B[(size_t)br*N+bc] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    #pragma unroll\n    for (int kk=0;kk<K_TILE;kk++) {\n      double4 a4 = *reinterpret_cast<const double4*>(&As[kk*TILE + ty*MICRO]);\n      double4 b4 = *reinterpret_cast<const double4*>(&Bs[kk*TILE + tx*MICRO]);\n      const double* a = reinterpret_cast<const double*>(&a4);\n      const double* b = reinterpret_cast<const double*>(&b4);\n      #pragma unroll\n      for (int m=0;m<MICRO;m++)\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int n=0;n<MICRO;n++)\n          acc[m][n] += a[m]*b[n];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n  }\n  ...\n}\n```\nSince MICRO=4, a4/b4 directly one double4 each. The A tile is staged\n// transposed in shared memory so that both operands can be fetched with\n// 128-bit vector loads during the inner product. Every thread accumulates a\n// MICRO x MICRO register tile, which gives a high FMA-to-shared-load ratio.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace cuda_gemm {\nconstexpr int TILE = 32;\nconstexpr int MICRO = 4;\nconstexpr int K_TILE = 64;\nconstexpr int THREADS = (TILE / MICRO) * (TILE / MICRO);\nconstexpr size_t SHARED_BYTES =\n    static_cast<size_t>(2) * K_TILE * TILE * sizeof(double);\n\nunion Vec4 {\n    double4 v;\n    double s[4];\n};\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS) void matmulKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n    double* __restrict__ C, int M, int N, int K) {\n    extern __shared__ double smem[];\n    // A is stored transposed: smem[c * TILE + r] == A[row0 + r][k0 + c].\n    double* As = smem;\n    double* Bs = smem + K_TILE * TILE;\n\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x % (TILE / MICRO);\n    const int warpRow = threadIdx.x / (TILE / MICRO);\n    const int row0 = blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const int col0 = blockIdx.x * TILE;\n\n    double acc[MICRO][MICRO];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < MICRO; ++m)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < MICRO; ++n) acc[m][n] = 0.0;\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < K; k0 += K_TILE) {\n        // Stage A (transposed) and B into shared memory.\n        for (int e = threadIdx.x; e < TILE * K_TILE; e += THREADS) {\n            const int r = e / K_TILE;\n            const int c = e % K_TILE;\n            const int gr = row0 + r;\n            const int gc = k0 + c;\n            As[c * TILE + r] =\n                (gr < M && gc < K) ? A[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * K + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        for (int e = threadIdx.x; e < K_TILE * TILE; e += THREADS) {\n            const int r = e / TILE;\n            const int c = e % TILE;\n            const int br = k0 + r;\n            const int bc = col0 + c;\n            Bs[r * TILE + c] =\n                (br < K && bc < N) ? B[static_cast<size_t>(br) * N + bc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int kk = 0; kk < K_TILE; ++kk) {\n            Vec4 a;\n            Vec4 b;\n            a.v = *reinterpret_cast<const double4*>(\n                &As[kk * TILE + warpRow * MICRO]);\n            b.v = *reinterpret_cast<const double4*>(\n                &Bs[kk * TILE + lane * MICRO]);\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < MICRO; ++m)\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int n = 0; n < MICRO; ++n)\n                    acc[m][n] += a.s[m] * b.s[n];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < MICRO; ++m) {\n        const int gr = row0 + warpRow * MICRO + m;\n        if (gr < M) {\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int n = 0; n < MICRO; ++n) {\n                const int gc = col0 + lane * MICRO + n;\n                if (gc < N)\n                    C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] = acc[m][n];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n}  // namespace cuda_gemm\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device management: the output rows are partitioned across all available\n// GPUs. Every device receives a full copy of B and the matching slice of A,\n// so each output element is still accumulated in the original k order.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nstruct GpuDevice {\n    int id = 0;\n    size_t rowStart = 0;\n    size_t rows = 0;\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    double* dB = nullptr;\n    double* dC = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\nstd::vector<GpuDevice> gDevices;\nsize_t gPreparedN = 0;\n\nvoid cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {\n    if (err != cudaSuccess) {\n        std::fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error (%s): %s\\n\", what,\n                     cudaGetErrorString(err));\n        std::exit(1);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid releaseDevices() {\n    for (GpuDevice& d : gDevices) {\n        cudaSetDevice(d.id);\n        if (d.dA) cudaFree(d.dA);\n        if (d.dB) cudaFree(d.dB);\n        if (d.dC) cudaFree(d.dC);\n        if (d.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(d.stream);\n    }\n    gDevices.clear();\n    gPreparedN = 0;\n}\n\n// Pick the number of GPUs that can actually shorten the critical path. If the\n// problem is too small to keep one GPU busy for more than a wave or two,\n// splitting it further only adds transfer overhead.\nint chooseDeviceCount(int deviceCount, size_t N) {\n    const size_t tiles =\n        (N + cuda_gemm::TILE - 1) / cuda_gemm::TILE;\n    const size_t totalTiles = tiles * tiles;\n\n    cudaDeviceProp prop{};\n    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, 0), \"get device properties\");\n    const int blocksPerSm = 3;  // 2 warps/block, 32 KiB shared per block\n    const size_t capacity =\n        static_cast<size_t>(prop.multiProcessorCount) * blocksPerSm;\n\n    if (totalTiles <= 2 * capacity) return 1;\n    return deviceCount;\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nvoid setupGpu(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n              const size_t N) {\n    if (gPreparedN == N && !gDevices.empty()) return;\n    releaseDevices();\n    gPreparedN = N;\n    if (N == 0) return;\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), \"get device count\");\n    if (deviceCount <= 0) {\n        std::fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error: no CUDA-capable device found\\n\");\n        std::exit(1);\n    }\n\n    const int usedDevices = chooseDeviceCount(deviceCount, N);\n\n    cudaCheck(cudaFuncSetAttribute(\n                  reinterpret_cast<const void*>(cuda_gemm::matmulKernel),\n                  cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,\n                  static_cast<int>(cuda_gemm::SHARED_BYTES)),\n              \"set shared memory attribute\");\n\n    gDevices.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(usedDevices));\n    size_t rowStart = 0;\n    const size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<size_t>(usedDevices);\n    const size_t remainder = N % static_cast<size_t>(usedDevices);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < usedDevices; ++i) {\n        const size_t rows =\n            baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(i) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n        if (rows == 0) continue;\n\n        GpuDevice d;\n        d.id = i;\n        d.rowStart = rowStart;\n        d.rows = rows;\n\n        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(i), \"set device\");\n        const size_t aElems = rows * N;\n        const size_t bElems = N * N;\n        if (aElems > 0)\n            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d.dA, aElems * sizeof(double)), \"malloc A\");\n        if (bElems > 0)\n            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d.dB, bElems * sizeof(double)), \"malloc B\");\n        if (aElems > 0)\n            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d.dC, aElems * sizeof(double)), \"malloc C\");\n        cudaCheck(cudaStreamCreate(&d.stream), \"create stream\");\n\n        if (aElems > 0)\n            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d.dA, A.data() + rowStart * N,\n                                 aElems * sizeof(double),\n                                 cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),\n                      \"upload A\");\n        if (bElems > 0)\n            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d.dB, B.data(), bElems * sizeof(double),\n                                 cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),\n                      \"upload B\");\n\n        gDevices.push_back(d);\n        rowStart += rows;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n    if (gPreparedN != N || gDevices.empty()) setupGpu(A, B, N);\n\n    // Launch every device first so their kernels execute concurrently.\n    for (const GpuDevice& d : gDevices) {\n        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(d.id), \"set device\");\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + cuda_gemm::TILE - 1) /\n                                              cuda_gemm::TILE),\n                        static_cast<unsigned>((d.rows + cuda_gemm::TILE - 1) /\n                                              cuda_gemm::TILE),\n                        1);\n        cuda_gemm::matmulKernel<<<grid, cuda_gemm::THREADS,\n                                  cuda_gemm::SHARED_BYTES, d.stream>>>(\n            d.dA, d.dB, d.dC, static_cast<int>(d.rows), static_cast<int>(N),\n            static_cast<int>(N));\n        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), \"launch matmul kernel\");\n    }\n\n    // Retrieve the result slices; the async copies overlap with the remaining\n    // devices while they are still computing.\n    for (const GpuDevice& d : gDevices) {\n        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(d.id), \"set device\");\n        const size_t elems = d.rows * N;\n        if (elems > 0)\n            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + d.rowStart * N, d.dC,\n                                      elems * sizeof(double),\n                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, d.stream),\n                      \"download C\");\n    }\n    for (const GpuDevice& d : gDevices) {\n        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(d.id), \"set device\");\n        cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(d.stream), \"synchronize stream\");\n    }\n}\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\nvoid initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n            \n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            \n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n            \n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Allocate matrices\n    std::vector<double> A(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(N * N);\n    \n    // Initialize matrices\n    printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrix(A, N);\n    initMatrix(B, N);\n\n    // Prepare device buffers before the timed region (allocation + upload are\n    // the GPU equivalent of the host-side matrix allocation above).\n    setupGpu(A, B, N);\n    \n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate GFLOPS\n    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(C, \"MatrixC\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            releaseDevices();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            releaseDevices();\n The A tile is staged\n// transposed in shared memory so that both operands can be fetched with\n// 128-bit vector loads during the inner product. The A tile is staged\n// transposed in shared memory so that both operands can be fetched with\n// 128-bit vector loads during the inner product. Every thread accumulates a\n// MICRO x MICRO register tile, which gives a high FMA-to-shared-load ratio.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace cuda_gemm {\nconstexpr int TILE = 32;\nconstexpr int MICRO = 4;\nconstexpr int K_TILE = 64;\nconstexpr int THREADS = (TILE / MICRO) * (TILE / MICRO);\nconstexpr size_t SHARED_BYTES =\n    static_cast<size_t>(2) * K_TILE * TILE * sizeof(double);\n\nunion Vec4 {\n    double4 v;\n    double s[4];\n};\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS) void matmulKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n    double* __restrict__ C, int M, int N, int K) {\n    extern __shared__ double smem[];\n    // A is stored transposed: smem[c * TILE + r] == A[row0 + r][k0 + c].\n    double* As = smem;\n    double* Bs = smem + K_TILE * TILE;\n\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x % (TILE / MICRO);\n    const int warpRow = threadIdx.x / (TILE / MICRO);\n    const int row0 = blockIdx.y * TILE;\n    const int col0 = blockIdx.x * TILE;\n\n    double acc[MICRO][MICRO];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < MICRO; ++m)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < MICRO; ++n) acc[m][n] = 0.0;\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < K; k0 += K_TILE) {\n        // Stage A (transposed) and B into shared memory.\n        for (int e = threadIdx.x; e < TILE * K_TILE; e += THREADS) {\n            const int r = e / K_TILE;\n            const int c = e % K_TILE;\n            const int gr = row0 + r;\n            const int gc = k0 + c;\n            As[c * TILE + r] =\n                (gr < M && gc < K) ? A[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * K + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        for (int e = threadIdx.x; e < K_TILE * TILE; e += THREADS) {\n            const int r = e / TILE;\n            const int c = e % TILE;\n            const int br = k0 + r;\n            const int bc = col0 + c;\n            Bs[r * TILE + c] =\n                (br < K && bc < N) ? B[static_cast<size_t>(br) * N + bc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int kk = 0; kk < K_TILE; ++kk) {\n            Vec4 a;\n            Vec4 b;\n            a.v = *reinterpret_cast<const double4*>(\n                &As[kk * TILE + warpRow * MICRO]);\n            b.v = *reinterpret_cast<const double4*>(\n                &Bs[kk * TILE + lane * MICRO]);\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < MICRO; ++m)\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int n = 0; n < MICRO; ++n)\n                    acc[m][n] += a.s[m] * b.s[n];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < MICRO; ++m) {\n        const int gr = row0 + warpRow * MICRO + m;\n        if (gr < M) {\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int n = 0; n < MICRO; ++n) {\n                const int gc = col0 + lane * MICRO + n;\n                if (gc < N)\n                    C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] = acc[m][n];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n}  // namespace cuda_gemm\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device management: the output rows are partitioned across all available\n// GPUs. Every device receives a full copy of B and the matching slice of A,\n// so each output element is still accumulated in the original k order.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nstruct GpuDevice {\n    int id = 0;\n    size_t rowStart = 0;\n    size_t rows = 0;\n    double* dA = nullptr;\nsize_t gRegisteredBytes = 0;\n\nvoid cudaCheck(cudaError_t err, const char* what) {\n    if (err != cudaSuccess) {\n        std::fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error (%s): %s\\n\", what,\n                     cudaGetErrorString(err));\n        std::exit(1);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid releaseDevices() {\n    for (GpuDevice& d : gDevices) {\n        cudaSetDevice(d.id);\n        if (d.dA) cudaFree(d.dA);\n        if (d.dB) cudaFree(d.dB);\n        if (d.dC) cudaFree(d.dC);\n        if (d.stream) cudaStreamDestroy(d.stream);\n    }\n    gDevices.clear();\n    if (gRegisteredC) {\n        cudaHostUnregister(gRegisteredC);\n        gRegisteredC = nullptr;\n        gRegisteredBytes = 0;\n    }\n    gPreparedN = 0;\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\n// Drop the pinned-output registration once the timed computation is over.\nvoid releaseOutputPin() {\n    if (gRegisteredC) {\n        cudaHostUnregister(gRegisteredC);\n        gRegisteredC = nullptr;\n        gRegisteredBytes = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid setupGpu(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n              const size_t N) {\n    if (gPreparedN == N && !gDevices.empty()) return;\n    releaseDevices();\n    gPreparedN = N;\n    if (N == 0) return;\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), \"get device count\");\n    if (deviceCount <= 0) {\n        std::fprintf(stderr, cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d.dB, B.data(), bElems * sizeof(double),\n                                 cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),\n                      \"upload B\");\n\n        gDevices.push_back(d);\n             static_cast<unsigned>((d.rows + cuda_gemm::TILE - 1) /\n                                              cuda_gemm::TILE),\n                        1);\n cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, d.stream),\n                      \"download C\");\n    }\n    for (const GpuDevice& d : gDevices) {\n        cudaCheck(cudaSetDevice(d.id), \"set device\");\n        cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(d.stream), \"synchronize stream\");\n    }\n}\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\nvoid initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n  return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n
